Conclusion: 恢复水印判决所需的是“足够好的”高SNR潜在统计而非完整逆扩散轨迹。通过锚点+残差分解和扩展流匹配的一步预测，可在保持感知质量与判别正确率的同时大幅降低计算开销，为扩散水印提取提供高效、可并行且具理论依据的方案。

Abstract: Semantic watermarks for diffusion models embed ownership information into the generative process while preserving perceptual quality, but Gaussian-Shading extraction conventionally requires multi-step diffusion inversion to recover the initial noise. Recent one-step methods show that this cost can be reduced substantially. We study this problem through extended flow matching and conditional regression. The key observation is that, near the high-SNR image endpoint, recovering a useful noise statistic given by the first-step output of the extended flow matching in the high-SNR regime is much simpler than reconstructing the full inverse trajectory, and Gaussian Shading only requires the recovered latent to remain in the correct watermark decision region. Based on this observation, we propose a lightweight, prompt-free extractor that decomposes endpoint recovery into an image-like anchor and a noise-oriented residual, which increases the capability of the model to utilize GPU parallel computation. The resulting method avoids iterative inversion and repeated evaluation of a diffusion-scale U-Net, providing an efficient one-step extraction pipeline with a concise theoretical interpretation. The computational cost of extracting noise is lower than that of both OSI and FARI. The github repo is there: https://github.com/TheLovesOfLadyPurple/RAIN-lightweight-NN-for-one-step-semantic-watermark-extraction

</details>


### [133] [What Makes a 3D Scene Editable? A Factorized Benchmark of Fidelity, Locality, Consistency, and Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14899)
*Sariah Patro,Arjun Mehra,Nikhil Bhatia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditBench3D提出一个与表示无关的3D场景编辑评测基准，从“受控信息替换”视角衡量编辑质量，覆盖指令忠实度、空间局限性、跨视角一致性与非目标内容保持，实验表明语义忠实度与其他维度弱相关，不同方法在多维权衡上各有优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前3D场景编辑常仅以语义对齐评估，但实际编辑可能误改无关区域、视角不一致或破坏原内容，单一语义分数无法反映编辑可控性与稳健性，需要一个能够全面刻画编辑属性的基准。

Method: 提出EditBench3D基准：将编辑视为“受控信息替换”，设计四项指标（指令忠实度、空间局部性、跨视角一致性、非目标内容保持）；采用可见性感知的3D目标支撑、成对文本描述、保留的相机视角，以及覆盖外观、材质、几何、对象级的五类编辑任务；对8种代表性编辑器（NeRF、3DGS、混合、代理/网格操控）在240组场景-编辑对上统一评测。

Result: 实验发现：语义（指令）忠实度与其他编辑属性仅弱相关；不存在在四维上同时最优的方法。显式高斯（3DGS）编辑器整体权衡最好；直接代理/几何操控法编辑最“保守”，更好地保护非目标区域，但在开放式语义忠实度上受限。

Conclusion: 编辑可评性应被报告为多目标画像而非单一语义分数；EditBench3D为不同表示和方法提供统一、可复现的多维评测框架，有助于推动更可控、更一致的3D场景编辑方法发展。

Abstract: Neural 3D scene editing is often evaluated by semantic alignment alone, although a convincing result may alter unrelated content or become inconsistent across views. We introduce EditBench3D, a representation-agnostic benchmark that treats editing as controlled information replacement. It evaluates four complementary properties: instruction fidelity, spatial locality, cross-view consistency, and preservation of non-target content. The protocol combines visibility-aware 3D target supports, paired descriptions, held-out cameras, and five edit families covering appearance, material, geometry, and object-level changes. We evaluate eight representative NeRF, 3D Gaussian Splatting, hybrid, and proxy-based editors on 240 scene-edit pairs. The study shows that semantic fidelity is only weakly associated with the other editing properties, and that no single method is optimal across all dimensions. Explicit Gaussian editors offer a strong overall balance, whereas direct proxy manipulation provides the most conservative edits at the cost of open-ended fidelity. These findings support reporting editability as a multi-objective profile rather than a single semantic score.

</details>


### [134] [ThreshGuide: Class-Aware Labeled-Guided Thresholding for Semi-Supervised 3D Abdominal Multi-Organ Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14943)
*Hongyu Liu,Yinlong Wang,Lusha Li,Hui Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ThreshGuide：一种类感知自适应阈值的伪标签方法，通过教师模型在有标注数据上估计每类最优阈值（最大化误差感知Fη），用EMA平滑后用于无标注体素筛选，在多器官分割中显著提升难学器官表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定全局置信阈值在腹部多器官分割中不适用，因为各器官大小、形态、外观与学习难度差异大，导致伪标签选择偏差，影响半监督分割效果。需要一种能按类别动态调节阈值的机制。

Method: 采用教师-学生框架。训练过程中，教师模型在有标注样本上评估不同阈值下的精度-覆盖权衡，以最大化误差感知的Fη指标来得到每个类别的目标阈值；再用指数滑动平均(EMA)对这些目标阈值进行时序平滑；最终在无标注数据上按类别使用该阈值过滤体素，生成更可靠的伪标签供学生学习。

Result: 在FLARE2022与AMOS2022数据集上整体性能具有竞争力，尤其对难学器官取得明显提升，相比固定全局阈值更稳健。

Conclusion: 类感知、由标注数据引导的阈值自适应能有效改进半监督医学图像分割的伪标签质量，缓解器官间差异带来的阈值敏感性问题，提升难学类别的分割表现。

Abstract: Pseudo-labeling is a strong paradigm for semi-supervised medical image segmentation, yet its effectiveness is highly sensitive to confidence thresholding. In abdominal multi-organ segmentation, a fixed global threshold is particularly suboptimal because organ classes differ substantially in size, appearance, and learning difficulty. In this work, we propose ThreshGuide, a class-aware threshold adaptation framework that uses labeled data to guide pseudo-label selection on unlabeled data. Built upon a standard teacher-student architecture, the teacher model evaluates labeled samples during training to estimate class-aware threshold targets by maximizing an error-aware F\b{eta} criterion that balances precision and coverage. These targets are then smoothed with an exponential moving average (EMA) and used to filter unlabeled voxels in a class-dependent manner. Experiments on FLARE2022 and AMOS2022 show that ThreshGuide performs competitively overall, yielding clear improvements specifically on hard-to-learn organs.

</details>


### [135] [MoVT: Video-Augmented Motion Tokenizer for Text-to-Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14965)
*Beibei Jing,Tianle Guo,Youjia Zhang,Zikai Song,Yawei Luo,Junqing Yu,Tao Guan,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoVT通过跨模态增强的运动分词器，把3D离散运动token投影到2D视频域以吸收海量视频中的复杂动作模式，再映射回3D，形成对齐且更丰富的2D/3D码本，并以生成式掩码Transformer在模态无关的token空间中进行文本到动作生成，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本驱动的3D人体动作生成受限于稀缺的3D训练数据，难以覆盖真实世界中多样、无约束的文本与动作表达。作者希望利用丰富的2D人类动作视频资源，补足3D数据不足并提升模型对复杂动作的表达能力。

Method: 提出跨模态增强的运动分词器：将离散3D运动token投影到2D域，用大规模视频学习增强并扩展运动码本的表达；随后将增强后的token映回3D，得到对齐的2D/3D码本。基于此，设计生成式掩码Transformer，在模态无关的token索引空间预测被掩码的运动token。通过来自标注视频的文本—2D索引对进一步训练生成器。

Result: 在多项关键指标上全面优于先前SOTA，证明利用2D视频增强码本与模态无关token预测的有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 跨模态码本对齐与掩码生成框架能有效将视频规模优势转移到3D动作生成，缓解3D数据稀缺，提升复杂动作的文本到运动合成质量。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D human motion generation models face significant challenges in responding to diverse and unconstrained textual prompts, primarily due to the limited availability of 3D motion training data. To address this, we introduce MoVT, a novel framework that effectively leverages the extensive range of human action videos to enhance text-to-motion generation. At the core of our approach is the cross-modal augmented motion tokenizer, which projects discrete 3D motion tokens into the 2D domain. This projection allows us to enrich the motion codebook with complex, real-world motion patterns derived from videos. The enriched discrete tokens are then mapped back to the 3D domain, resulting in aligned 3D and 2D codebooks with an enhanced capacity to represent intricate motions. These enhanced codebooks are integrated into a generative masked transformer, which predicts masked motion token indices in a modality-agnostic manner. This enables the use of text-index pairs, generated from the 2D codebook and annotated motion videos, to further enhance the generator. Extensive empirical evaluations show that MoVT performs favorably against prior state-of-the-art methods across multiple key metrics.

</details>


### [136] [PhysBrain 1.5: From Vision-Language Models to Physical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14973)
*DeepCybo Team,Yu Bin,Haipeng Cao,Zheng Chang,Kai Chen,Youning Chen,Kailin Deng,Yichao Du,Xiaotong Fu,Haoyang Ge,Yunlong Guo,Chenliu Hao,Jiyan He,Xuguo He,Yakun Hou,Kai Hu,Cong Huang,Tuopusen Huang,Yu Huang,Hong Li,Peize Li,Shijie Lian,Xiaopeng Lin,Yun Lin,Haibao Liu,Haochen Liu,Qiuzhi Liu,Shengcai Liu,Zhiqiang Liu,Tao Luo,Peng Ren,Shuo Ren,Chaoyi Ruan,Zhaolong Shen,Yukun Shi,Qiyuan Su,Yuxuan Tian,Yining Wang,Changti Wu,Hao Wu,Xueyin Xu,Ruoqi Yang,Zhaoyang Yang,Hang Yuan,Zhaoyang Zeng,Hanwen Zhang,Ruimeng Zhang,Yao Zhang,Yibo Zhang,Yuxiang Zhang,Zhirui Zhang,Ziyi Zhang,Zubin Zheng,Zishen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysBrain 1.5 将“物理观察-交互-环境变化”的闭环整合进统一自回归序列建模框架，能理解场景、生成动作并预测未来状态；以人类交互视频为主预训练，再经示教/机器人/模拟混合微调，在28项具身理解基准上以8B模型达72.5分，开源SOTA，并能输出末端轨迹与多模态未来帧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界智能需要同时“看懂—能动—预见”。现有多模态/具身方法多分裂为理解、控制、预测三条线，语义与空间/动力学难统一，且缺少大规模具身监督。作者受“物理循环”启发，欲将语言理解、动作生成、未来场景预测统一到同一学习与推理接口中，提升泛化与可迁移性，并缩小开源与专有模型差距。

Method: 以通用视觉-语言模型为底座，将三类输出（语言响应、末端执行器运动、稠密视觉目标如RGB/深度/机器人掩膜）统一离散化为token序列，采用自回归下一个token预测进行多任务联合优化。预训练完全基于人类交互视频：构造以任务为中心的片段，配对语义与空间上下文、恢复的人体动作及后续观测；随后用监督微调，混合人类示教、机器人轨迹与模拟经验以适配具身控制。推理时可生成文本、动作序列与未来帧。

Result: 8B参数模型在28个具身理解基准平均72.5分，开源最优，14项基准排名开源第一，整体与GPT-6-Astra、Gemini 3.6 Flash等专有模型相当；同时保留一般多模态能力。定性结果展示了可生成末端轨迹与空间对齐的未来RGB/深度/机器人掩膜。

Conclusion: 将理解、控制与预测统一为离散自回归序列学习可显著提升具身理解与多任务泛化，且仅用人类交互视频即可获得强大预训练信号；经多源微调能在开源范围内达到或逼近专有SOTA，并具备可视化的未来场景与动作生成能力。

Abstract: We present PhysBrain 1.5, a unified model for understanding physical environments, generating actions, and predicting future states. Motivated by the physical loop of observation, interaction, and environmental change, we bring these capabilities into a common learning framework. Starting from a general vision--language model, we encode language responses, end-effector motion, and dense visual targets as discrete sequences and jointly optimize them with autoregressive next-token prediction. Pre-training draws its embodied supervision entirely from human interaction videos, using task-centered episodes to pair semantic and spatial context with recovered motion and subsequent observations. We then adapt the model through supervised fine-tuning on a mixture of human demonstrations, robot trajectories, and simulated experience. Across 28 embodied understanding benchmarks, our 8B model achieves an average score of 72.5, setting a new open-source state of the art and performing on par with leading proprietary models such as GPT-6-Astra and Gemini 3.6 Flash. It achieves the best open-source results on 14 benchmarks while retaining general multimodal capabilities. Beyond these understanding evaluations, qualitative examples show the model's ability to produce end-effector trajectories and predict future scenes through spatially aligned RGB, depth, and robot-mask outputs.

</details>


### [137] [G-ray: Ray-Level Relative Geometric Position Encoding in Multi-View Vision Transformers under Camera Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15018)
*Shuo Zhang,Xin Su,Wei Wang,Jun Liu,Xinrui Zeng,Yongsen Chen,Chenjie Wang,Guibo Zhu,Jinqiao Wang,Bin Luo,Liangpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出G-ray：一种以“相机局部光线角”参数化的旋转相对位置编码，使多视角Transformer在不同成像模型/视场下获得投影不变的一致几何线索，显著提升异构相机下的3D重建与新视角合成表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RoPE等相对位置编码多以图像平面坐标定义，相位随投影模型和FoV变化而改变，导致跨投影注意力的几何线索不一致；多相机异构（针孔/非针孔、不同FoV）场景下，这种不一致显著削弱多视角Transformer的融合与泛化能力。

Method: 将旋转相位由“像素坐标差”改为“相机坐标系下的射线（光线）夹角/方向”来参数化：同一相机局部射线对在不同投影模型下诱导相同相位，实现投影不变的相对编码。G-ray可直接作为编码或与RoPE/GTA/RayRoPE等现有方法并用，无需新增可学习参数。

Result: 在3个异构3D重建基准（50视角）上，集成G-ray的模型在6项均值指标均领先；相较MapAnything（两者均给定标定），平均pointmap相对误差下降45.8%。仅用同构针孔数据训练的模型能在测试时处理针孔+非针孔混合输入而不需再训练，并在纯针孔协议上仍具竞争力。于NVS任务，在视点与FoV联变条件下，GTA与RayRoPE结合G-ray均进一步提升。

Conclusion: 以光线为原语的相对位置编码提供投影不变、相机异构稳健的几何先验，可无缝叠加到现有RoPE类方法并带来显著收益，对多投影、多FoV场景的3D重建与NVS具有实用价值。

Abstract: We study relative position encoding for multi-view vision Transformers under camera heterogeneity, including varying fields of view (FoVs) or projection models. Existing rotary relative position encodings commonly use image-plane positional coordinates, producing projection-dependent relative phases and inconsistent geometric cues for cross-projection attention. We introduce G-ray, a ray-level relative position encoding whose rotary phases are parameterized by camera-local ray angles. The same camera-local ray pair induces the same relative phase across projections, providing projection-invariant positional consistency. G-ray can be used directly or integrated with existing encodings, retaining complementary geometric cues without additional learned parameters. We validate G-ray in three host encodings, RoPE, GTA, and RayRoPE, across 3D reconstruction and novel-view synthesis (NVS). Across three heterogeneous 3D reconstruction benchmarks at 50 views, G-ray leads all six averaged metrics and reduces mean pointmap relative error by 45.8% over MapAnything, with calibration supplied to both. Trained exclusively on homogeneous pinhole images, the 3D reconstruction model handles mixed pinhole and non-pinhole inputs without retraining and remains competitive on homogeneous pinhole 3D reconstruction protocols. For NVS, GTA and RayRoPE improve with G-ray under joint viewpoint and FoV variation. The project's webpage is available at https://g-ray-project.github.io/.

</details>


### [138] [TwinICL: Diagnosing Multimodal In-Context Learning through Paired Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15028)
*Zihan Xue,Po-Yi Lu,Serhii Honcharenko,Zih-Ching Chen,Hsuan-Tien Lin,Nanyun Peng,I-Hung Hsu,Kuan-Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TwinICL 提供成对匹配的文本与图像版本任务，用于严格对比多模态与纯文本的上下文学习；结果显示多模态 ICL 普遍落后，但通过针对视觉接入、任务表述与推理的联合干预，可在诊断子集上显著缩小差距；即便给出明确指令仍存在模态差距，演示样例既是处理负担也是任务证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ICL 基准难以跨模态公平比较，因为缺少内容与结构严格对齐的文本版与图像版任务；社区也不清楚多模态 ICL 劣势源于任务推断失败还是执行困难，以及演示样例在多模态情境中的具体作用。

Method: 构建 procedurally generated 的 TwinICL，生成文本/图像双生任务对，覆盖38个任务族；评测6个开源多模态模型与文本模型的 ICL 表现；设计三类干预：提升视觉访问（如更清晰呈现/对齐视觉信息）、优化任务框架（提示格式/说明）、加强推理（链式/分步）；再在“有明确指令”的设定下区分“任务推断”与“任务执行”来源的误差；分析加入示例输入/输出对模态差距的影响。

Result: 跨模型与任务族，多模态 ICL 一致性弱于文本 ICL，差距因任务而异；单一干预效果不稳定，但三者联用能在诊断子集上恢复到强多模态 ICL 表现；即使提供明确任务指令，模态差距依然存在；示例既增加认知处理负担，又提供任务证据，二者权衡会改变模态差距。

Conclusion: TwinICL 证明当前多模态模型在 ICL 上存在系统性短板，不仅在任务推断，也在执行环节；综合性提示与推理干预可部分弥补，但并非通用解；基准为未来多模态 ICL 方法与模型改进提供了可控、可复现实验平台。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables models to infer tasks from demonstrations, but existing benchmarks generally lack matched text and image versions needed to compare ICL performance across modalities. We introduce TwinICL, a procedurally generated benchmark providing such pairs for controlled comparison. Across six open-weight models and 38 tasks, multimodal ICL consistently underperforms text-only ICL, with gaps varying by task family. To test whether this gap can be recovered, we target visual access, task framing, and reasoning through three interventions. Their combination recovers strong multimodal ICL performance on a diagnostic subset, despite limited or inconsistent individual effects. To distinguish difficulties in executing tasks from those in inferring them, we evaluate models with explicit task instructions, revealing a modality gap even when the task is known. We then examine how adding demonstration inputs and outputs reshapes this gap, highlighting demonstrations' dual role as additional context to process and evidence about the task. The dataset is available at https://github.com/lab-flair/TwinICL.

</details>


### [139] [Tele360: Real-Time Feed-Forward Human Reconstruction from Sparse Unposed Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15032)
*Hanzhang Tu,Zhanfeng Liao,Wei Min,Jiajun Zhang,Yebin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tele360提出首个实时前馈系统，从稀疏、未标定RGB流同时估计相机姿态并重建动态3D高斯表示，实现直播式自由视点人像渲染，2K分辨率>25FPS，质量SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自由视点动态人重建方法要么优化开销大、难以实时；要么需标定相机和低清输入，限制高分辨率在线应用。需要一种在稀疏、未配准多视角下仍可实时、端到端重建与渲染的方法。

Method: 提出Tele360端到端前馈管线：1）稀疏感知多视图Transformer主干，前景人体token化并以共享scene token保留全局上下文；2）全Transformer高斯解码器，避免卷积过平滑并保持稀疏高效；3）混合特征金字塔，将多尺度外观特征注入几何预测；4）轻量可微LM相机精炼层，提升多视一致与几何对齐；5）通过大规模视觉-几何基础模型的教师-学生蒸馏迁移多视几何先验；6）将预测的高斯maps经视频编解码流式传输至终端进行交互式自由视点渲染。

Result: 在工作室基准上达到SOTA视觉质量；单张消费级GPU上实现2K输入端到端渲染>25 FPS；在多主体、服饰、动作与多相机设置下的自采数据上表现稳健。

Conclusion: Tele360证明了在稀疏、未标定多视视频下，实时前馈的人体动态重建与自由视点可视化可行，通过Transformer架构、高斯表示、可微LM精炼与蒸馏等设计，在质量与实时性上同时达标，适合远程沉浸式通信与交互应用。

Abstract: Live free-viewpoint visualization of real humans is critical for immersive communication and interactive digital experiences. Existing methods either rely on computationally expensive optimization or require calibrated cameras and low-resolution inputs, making real-time high-resolution deployment impractical. In this work, we present Tele360, the first real-time feed-forward system for dynamic human reconstruction and live free-viewpoint visualization from sparse, unposed RGB streams. Our system jointly estimates camera poses and reconstructs a dynamic 3D Gaussian representation for each time instance in a single forward pass. To achieve this, we start by designing a lightweight sparsity-aware multi-view transformer backbone that tokenizes foreground human regions while preserving global context through a shared scene token. We then employ a fully transformer-based Gaussian decoder to mitigate convolution-induced over-smoothing while keeping decoding sparse and efficient. In addition, we introduce a hybrid feature pyramid that injects multi-scale appearance cues into geometry prediction. We further introduce a lightweight differentiable Levenberg-Marquardt camera refinement layer to enhance multi-view consistency and geometric alignment. Moreover, to stabilize learning under sparse, unposed inputs, we transfer multi-view geometry priors from a large visual-geometry foundation model via teacher-student distillation. Finally, the predicted Gaussian maps are streamed with video codecs to remote devices for interactive free-viewpoint rendering. Extensive experiments show that Tele360 achieves state-of-the-art visual quality on studio benchmarks while supporting real-time 2K input-to-rendering at over 25 FPS on a single consumer GPU. Additional captured sequences illustrate its performance across varied subjects, clothing, and motions under our multi-camera setup.

</details>


### [140] [LG-VLN: A Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation Framework with LangGraph State Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15098)
*Jianhe Zhao,Yanhua Qiu,Zhiyu Zhang,Zibo Zhao,Jinhua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LG-VLN：仅用单目RGB、零样本的VLN-CE系统，通过共享视觉特征与LangGraph状态编排，在R2R-CE val-unseen子集上取得21.3%成功率/12.1%SPL，展示共享表示+显式状态机对连续导航有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN-CE多依赖LiDAR/全景/额外传感器，且将几何建图与语义导航割裂，导致长轨迹中空间-语义不一致与累积误差。需要一种仅用单目RGB、统一表示并具备鲁棒状态管理的方案，实现零样本泛化。

Method: 1) 在线前馈单目3D重建：预测深度、相机位姿与稠密点云，用于位姿估计与全局地图融合。2) 共享稠密CleanDIFT特征用于几何与导航；以语义一致性过滤跨帧误匹配，并用目标实例约束构建视觉参考。3) 语义价值图：将视觉相似度与局部BLIP-2图文相关性融合，得到目标相关性评分。4) LangGraph：将指令解析、几何感知、语义更新、路径规划、动作执行与失败恢复建模为有向状态图，具条件转移、持久状态与模块化恢复。

Result: 在R2R-CE val-unseen的固定550个episode子集上，成功率21.3%，SPL 12.1%。消融显示：共享语义特征显著提升导航性能，进一步与图文相关性融合可继续提升。

Conclusion: 共享视觉表示与显式状态编排在仅用单目RGB的零样本VLN-CE中有效；LG-VLN无需额外传感器即可取得有竞争力结果，验证了几何-语义一体化与LangGraph编排的价值，并具可复现性（代码将公开）。

Abstract: Continuous-environment vision-and-language navigation (VLN-CE) requires interpreting natural-language instructions in unseen 3D environments and executing continuous low-level actions. Existing methods often depend on LiDAR, panoramic cameras, or extra sensors; separate geometric-mapping and semantic-navigation visual representations can cause long-trajectory spatial-semantic inconsistencies. We propose LG-VLN, a monocular zero-shot framework with shared visual features and LangGraph-based state orchestration. An online feed-forward 3D reconstruction network predicts depth, camera poses, and dense point clouds for agent-pose estimation and global map fusion. Geometry and navigation share dense CleanDIFT features: semantic consistency rejects incorrect inter-frame correspondences, while target-instance constraints define visual references whose similarity combines with local BLIP-2 image-text relevance to form a semantic value map. LangGraph represents instruction parsing, geometric perception, semantic value updates, path planning, action execution, and failure recovery as a directed state graph with conditional transitions, persistent state, and modular recovery mechanisms. On a fixed 550-episode subset of the R2R-CE val-unseen split, LG-VLN achieves 21.3% success and 12.1% success weighted by path length. Ablations show shared semantic features improve navigation, further boosted by combining visual similarity and image-text relevance. Results establish shared visual representations and explicit state orchestration as effective for zero-shot VLN-CE using monocular RGB alone. Code will be publicly released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [141] [ChatGPT Images 2.5 in the Wild: A Launch-Period Dataset and Detector Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15100)
*Dennis Ng,Xingyu Shen,Ankit Raj,Kidus Zewde,Tommy Duong,Yuchen Zhou,Yuxin Zhang,Neo Tiangratanakul,Simiao Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究在图像工具更换底层生成器且保留同名时，网络帖文中的版本归因歧义；基于ChatGPT Images 2.5 发布后前51.1小时内收集的3,478张图像评估多种检测器表现，发现来源构成强烈影响内容分布，且现有冻结检测器在该集合上的标记率与误报率差异巨大，无法据此简单判断优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成平台可在保留产品名的同时更换底层模型，导致社媒/平台帖文中的“模型归因”不可靠；在产品过渡期（如ChatGPT Images 2.5 上线）社区声称的使用模型与实际生成器可能不一致，影响对模型能力、内容生态与检测器评测的解读。

Method: - 构建“冻结”数据集：来自8个来源的2,440帖，共3,478张图；时间窗口为公告后51.1小时内。
- 记录三层归因（如标题/说明的声称、平台宿主记录等），并通过图像表面过滤与定向复核保留独立图像；不独立验证生成器身份。
- 分析来源混合对内容画像的影响（如CLIP分配的题材类别）。
- 评测6个冻结检测器：在“参考照片”上以5%标记率标定阈值，然后报告该集合上的整体标记率；并在留出艺术作品上测量误报率；进行X平台四月与九月集合的探索性对比（固定阈值、帖簇自助法置信区间）。

Result: - NightCafe占39.0%图像，却贡献了CLIP判定幻想场景的77.0%，显示来源强烈偏置内容类型。
- 在以5%照片标记率标定阈值下，六个检测器对本集合的标记率为3.7%到56.4%，比GenImage召回低42–81个百分点。
- 留出艺术作品的误报率范围极广：1.5%到96.5%，因此较高的集合标记率并不意味着更好检测。
- 四月与九月集合的X-only探索性对比：Effort的九月标记率更高，DoU显示提示性差异；但因归因、内容、处理差异，不能作因果解释。

Conclusion: 产品过渡期中，公开帖文对“使用了哪个模型”的归因并不可靠，来源构成与内容分布深度交织，给生成检测基准带来偏差。现有冻结检测器在该真实世界集合上表现分化且误报严重，单靠总体标记率不足以评估优劣。所构建的数据集保留来源与归因证据，可用于分析产品切换期间的“声称模型使用”与检测表现，但需谨慎解读，避免因归因不确定与内容混杂而做出因果结论。

Abstract: An image tool can change its underlying generator while retaining its public name, making version attribution from online posts ambiguous. We study this problem after the ChatGPT Images 2.5 launch. Our frozen collection contains 3,478 images from 2,440 posts across 8 sources. Recorded posting times fall within the first 51.1 hours after the announcement. It records three attribution tiers and retains standalone images after image-form filtering and targeted review. Caption claims and host records provide admission evidence, not independently verified generator identity. The observed content profile depends on the source mixture: NightCafe supplies 39.0% of images but 77.0% of CLIP-assigned fantasy scenes. We then evaluate six frozen detectors at thresholds calibrated to a 5% flag rate on reference photographs. Collection flag rates range from 3.7 to 56.4%, falling 42-81 percentage points below GenImage recall. Held-out artwork false-positive rates range from 1.5 to 96.5%, so a higher collection flag rate does not by itself establish better detection. An exploratory X-only comparison with our April collection finds a higher September flag rate for Effort, and a suggestive difference for DoU, under fixed-threshold post-clustered bootstrap intervals. Attribution, content and processing differences prevent a causal interpretation of these contrasts. The collection supports analysis of reported model use during a product transition, with source and attribution evidence retained for interpretation.

</details>


### [142] [DNF-SR: Dual-Input and Negative-Aware Feature Fine-Tuning for Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15120)
*Shuhao Han,Wenjie Liao,Hayden Vance,Hang Dong,Rui Zhang,Chun-Le Guo,Chongyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DNF-SR：通过双输入与“负例感知”特征微调，实现一步扩散的真实世界超分既高保真又具感知一致性，并通过输出优选与策略改进进一步稳定提升质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步扩散可高效做Real-ISR，但直接把LR喂给训练于自然图像分布的扩散模型会产生分布鸿沟；给LR加噪声可缓解却会破坏内容。需在效率、保真与感知质量之间取得平衡，并利用扩散的随机性提升结果多样性与质量。

Method: 1) 双输入：将原始LR与加噪LR拼接输入到基于扩散的图像编辑模型，一步生成SR，既保留内容又缩小分布差异。2) 负例感知特征微调（NF^2T）：对多次采样得到的输出进行打分，划分为正/负子集；在图像与特征空间中定义隐式的“策略改进”方向，鼓励靠近正例、远离负例，从而后训练优化模型。

Result: 大量实验显示DNF-SR在真实世界超分上优于现有方法，在保真度、感知质量与稳定性方面均有提升；同时保持一步推理的效率。

Conclusion: 通过双输入减少分布鸿沟并保持内容一致，结合负例感知的后训练优化，DNF-SR在Real-ISR实现兼顾效率与高质量的超分，优于当下替代方案；代码将开源。

Abstract: Benefiting from the powerful generative priors of diffusion models, diffusion-based real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR) methods have demonstrated impressive performance.To achieve efficient Real-ISR, several recent works have designed one-step diffusion-based models.Howerver, unmediatedly feeding LR into a diffusion model creates a distributional gap with the model's original input.A straightforward approach to reduce the distribution gap is to introduce noise to the LR latents. However, directly adding noise inevitably corrupts the content of the LR images.In this study, we propose DNF-SR, a Dual-input and Negative-aware Feature fine-tuning method for Real-ISR.Specifically, we use a dual-input strategy that concatenates the original LR image with the noisy LR input and feeds them into a diffusion-based image editing model, ensuring both high-fidelity one-step super-resolution and improved perceptual and content consistency.Additionally, the noise present in the noisy LR input introduces randomness and diversity into the outputs. We exploit this property and propose a post-training optimization method, Negative-aware Feature Fine-Tuning (NF2T), which guides the model toward producing higher-quality results.NF^2T classifies multiple outputs into positive and negative subsets and then defines implicit policy improvement directions in both the image and feature spaces, thereby further enhancing the stability of the optimization.Extensive experiments show that DNF-SR outperforms other methods.Code will be released.

</details>


### [143] [AdaVSkip: Adaptive Visual Token Skipping Across Layers For Efficient MLLMs Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15131)
*Yuyao Sun,Tao Deng,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdaVSkip：为每层自注意与MLP各配一轻量路由器，按输入自适应决定视觉token是否跳过相应模块；用两阶段训练（监督+强化）只训练路由器、冻结骨干，显著减少视觉计算而保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM推理代价高，视觉token在所有层传播造成大量冗余。现有方法多做横向压缩（减token），或固定/早退的纵向跳层，但冗余在不同输入、且在自注意与MLP间的分布并不一致，需要更细粒度、输入自适应、模块级别的节省策略。

Method: 在每层引入两个独立路由器，分别判断视觉token是否通过自注意或MLP，从而形成输入特定的视觉计算路径。训练仅更新路由器、冻结骨干：阶段I基于模块必要性分数给出输入特定监督目标，进行有监督训练；阶段II采用强化学习，以答案正确性为主奖励，并加上跳过一致性惩罚以避免过度保留视觉计算，优化离散路由决策。

Result: 在三种MLLM骨干上验证：以LLaVA-NeXT-7B为例，单用AdaVSkip在保持平均任务性能的同时减少53.2% FLOPs；与视觉token压缩联合时，FLOPs降至91.2%，仍保留原始性能的97.2%（平均）。

Conclusion: 模块级自适应跳算可有效利用输入与模块间的纵向冗余，仅训练轻量路由器即可在不牺牲精度的前提下显著降耗，并与现有token压缩方法互补、可叠加带来更大加速。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) require substantial computation to process numerous visual tokens across all transformer layers. Most methods for efficient MLLM inference exploit horizontal redundancy by compressing visual tokens. Beyond token reduction, recent studies exploit vertical redundancy through early exit or fixed-layer skipping. However, we find that the extent and distribution of this redundancy vary across inputs and differ between self-attention and MLP modules. Motivated by these observations, we propose AdaVSkip, which equips each layer with two lightweight routers that independently determine whether visual tokens pass through by or skip the self-attention and MLP modules. These decisions collectively define an input-specific visual-computation path, but their discrete and non-differentiable nature makes learning effective paths challenging. To address this challenge, we develop a progressive two-stage training framework that updates only the routers while keeping the backbone frozen. Stage I establishes an initial routing policy through supervised training with input-specific targets derived from module-wise necessity scores. To further align the routing policy with task performance, Stage II uses reinforcement learning to optimize routing decisions with direct feedback from generated answers. It combines an answer correctness reward with a skip-consistency reward that discourages excessive retention of visual-token computation. Across three MLLM backbones, AdaVSkip maintains strong task performance with substantially less computation. On LLaVA-NeXT-7B, AdaVSkip reduces FLOPs by 53.2\% while preserving the original model's average performance. Combining it with visual token compression increases this reduction to 91.2\%, while retaining 97.2\% of the original performance on average.

</details>


### [144] [SparseTalk - Sparsifying 3D Gaussian Language Fields for Efficient 3D Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15137)
*Davit Soselia,Joseph JaJa,Amitabh Varshney*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究3D Gaussian 语言场在3D VQA中的语义冗余，提出将密集语义嵌入极端稀疏化（最低至8个视觉token），并通过多种选择策略验证，仅用数百token即可保持强性能。基于对象的稀疏化在256 token预算下几乎不损精度，同时大幅降低存储/内存/推理成本（特征内存降125倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集的3D Gaussian语言场虽有空间对齐优势，但需要数万语义嵌入，带来高存储、内存与推理代价。作者想回答：为下游3D VQA推理，究竟需要多少语义表示？是否存在极高冗余，能在不显著掉点的前提下将表示极度压缩？

Method: 以完整密集嵌入为起点，系统性地稀疏化语义嵌入，覆盖从单张图像等价块以下直至8个视觉token的极端低预算。比较多种token选择策略：随机、几何（空间/均匀）、语义（基于特征重要性）、空间-语义联合，以及新提出的基于对象的稀疏化（按检测到的对象实例分配token预算并保留背景上下文）。在ScanQA与MV-ScanQA上评测效率与精度。

Result: 实验证明密集语言场存在大量冗余：仅用数百个语义嵌入（<1%原表示）即可保持强3D VQA性能。对象式选择表现优异：在256 token预算下性能仅有轻微变化。SparseTalk在该预算时只保留SplatTalk推理输入的0.80%（32,076→256），相当于平均77,207个高斯的0.332%，推理吞吐量提升且解码特征内存降低125倍。

Conclusion: 3D Gaussian语言场可被大幅稀疏化而不显著损失VQA性能。对象感知的token分配在极低预算下最稳健，能以极小表示获得高效推理，显著降低存储/内存/计算开销，为3D多模态推理提供更轻量的表示范式。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian language fields provide an explicit, spatially grounded representation for 3D visual question answering (VQA), but their dense semantic features can require tens of thousands of embeddings per scene, resulting in substantial storage, memory, and inference costs. We investigate how much of this representation is actually necessary for downstream reasoning. Starting from a full embedding representation, we systematically sparsify its semantic embeddings, including the previously underexplored regime below a single image-equivalent block down to 8 visual tokens. We compare random, geometric, semantic, and joint spatial-semantic selection strategies and introduce an object-based sparsification method that distributes the token budget across detected object instances while retaining background context. Experiments on ScanQA and MV-ScanQA reveal substantial redundancy in dense Gaussian language fields. Strong VQA performance is retained with only a few hundred semantic embeddings, corresponding to less than 1% of the original representation. Object-based selection performs well relative to others, with only modest observed changes down to 256 tokens. At this budget, SparseTalk retains 0.80% of SplatTalk's 32,076-token inference input and 0.332% of the mean 77,207-Gaussian dense field, increasing inference throughput while reducing decoded-feature memory 125-fold.

</details>


### [145] [Automated Perceptually-Motivated Assessment of Photographic Consistency in Paired Clinical Photographs: Pipeline Development and Internal Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15144)
*Derrick Lin,Samantha Rabinovich,Joclin Rabinovich,Kassra Garoosi,Sumun Khetpal,Evan Delanoy,Neel Bhardwaj,Jason Roostaeian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 他们提出一个“照片一致性”总分S，用13个子指标、5个聚类维度综合评估整形外科术前/术后配对照片是否在可比条件下拍摄；在真实与扰动数据上表现稳健（AUC≈0.93）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术前/术后对照照是整形外科证据标准，但缺乏客观指标判断两张是否在一致的拍摄条件下拍摄，导致比较可能失真。需要一个可量化的、与人类感知一致的照片可比性度量。

Method: 构建感知驱动的管线：计算13个经标定的子指标，通过无监督相关结构分析分成5个簇（光度、纹理/清晰度、姿态、光照方向、俯仰），各簇内取均值，再按加权和得到总一致性分S。每个子指标标定使其在同一患者已发表配对中的中位差为0.5。用134组同患者配对校准，并在相同图像对、合成扰动对、以及134组跨出版错配对上评估。

Result: 总分S可有效区分匹配与不匹配（d' = 2.15，AUC = 0.928；均有95% CI）。三个头部姿态角未同簇：偏航与滚转同簇，俯仰独立。完全相同图片达顶分S=0.99；在五种扰动轴上，扰动越大S单调下降。

Conclusion: 该分数量化的是照片可比性而非美学或手术质量。作者提供了一个免费网页工具用于审核术前/术后照片的可比性，后续需与专家判读进行验证。

Abstract: Purpose: Paired pre- and post-operative photographs are the standard unit of evidence for plastic surgical outcomes, yet no objective metric verifies whether two images of the same patient were captured under conditions consistent for comparison.
  Approach: We developed a perceptually motivated pipeline that analyzes pre/post pairs across thirteen calibrated sub-metrics, partitioned by unsupervised correlation-structure analysis into five data-driven clusters (photometric, texture / sharpness, pose, illumination direction, and pitch), averaged within each cluster and combined across clusters by a weighted sum into a single consistency score. Each sub-metric is calibrated so that its median difference across published within-patient pairs scores 0.5, which is a reference point and carries no pass/fail meaning. The pipeline was calibrated on 134 matched within-patient published pre/post pairs and evaluated against identical-image pairs, synthetic-perturbation pairs, and 134 mismatched cross-publication pairs.
  Results: The master consistency score S separated matched from mismatched pairs (sensitivity index d' = 2.15, 95% confidence interval (CI) [1.83, 2.55]; area under the receiver operating characteristic curve AUC = 0.928, 95% CI [0.896, 0.959]), closely matching Gaussian-equal-variance predictions. The three head-pose angles did not fall in one cluster: yaw and roll grouped together while pitch separated. Identical pairs scored at ceiling (S = 0.99) and the master score fell monotonically with perturbation magnitude on all five perturbation axes.
  Conclusions: The score quantifies photographic comparability, not aesthetic or surgical quality, and provides a freely available web tool for auditing the photographic comparability of pre/post pairs, pending validation against expert judgment.

</details>


### [146] [Weakly Supervised Spatial Grounding for Discriminative Attention-Based Ultrasound-Histopathology Alignment in Prostate Cancer Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15150)
*Obed Korshie Dzikunu,Emma Willis,Mohammad Mahdi Abootorabi,Mohamed Harmanani,Zhuoxin Guo,Ferdinand Luger,Adam Kinnaird,Brian Wodlinger,Parvin Mousavi,Purang Abolmaesumi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出在微超声（micro-US）活检核心上，引入弱空间监督（基于病灶占比）来解耦“特征判别性学习”和“注意力选patch”的目标，从而改进与冷冻病理教师的非配对跨模态对齐；在7中心811名患者7166条活检核心上显著提升AUC，并证明仅靠注意力正则不足以替代标签驱动的监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往非配对跨模态蒸馏将微超声编码器与病理教师对齐，但一个对齐目标既要让patch特征可分又要决定哪些patch被汇聚，易导致注意力近乎均匀、难以聚焦关键病灶，从而限制临床关键任务（如临床显著性前列腺癌csPCa）的检测性能。

Method: 在活检层面利用常规记录的“恶性组织百分比（percentage involvement）”作为弱空间监督：1) 对编码器特征施加比例约束，独立于对齐损失，迫使核心内预测的恶性比例与标注一致；2) 冻结病理教师，保持按分级组（grade group）对应的跨模态对齐，仅对差异性的patch特征执行对齐；3) 以注意力汇聚针芯patch，但由于特征已被比例监督分化，注意力能集中于关键子区域而非塌缩为近均匀。

Result: 在7中心、811名患者、7166条活检核心、患者级五折交叉验证中：宏AUC 67.1、csPCa AUC 68.5；优于现有非配对对齐方法（61.2/52.8）与最强单模态基线（63.1/62.6）。消融实验证明：传统注意力正则仅限制注意力分布形状，不能替代基于标签的比例监督对特征层的约束。

Conclusion: 解耦目标并引入活检比例的弱监督，可使注意力聚焦病灶、提升跨模态蒸馏的效果，并在多中心前列腺活检数据上显著优于现有方法；注意力正则不是等价替代，关键在于对特征的标签驱动塑形。

Abstract: Unpaired cross-modal distillation transfers grade structure from histopathology into a micro-ultrasound (micro-US) encoder by aligning a pooled needle-region embedding to a frozen histopathology teacher under grade-group correspondence alone. A single objective is thereby required to serve two distinct functions: rendering patch features discriminative of tissue state, and selecting which patches enter the pooled representation. We decouple them. Weak spatial supervision derived from percentage involvement, recorded routinely at biopsy, constrains the predicted proportion of malignant tissue within each core, acting on the encoder features independently of the alignment objective. The alignment loss then operates on features that differ across a core, and attention concentrates on a subset of patches rather than remaining near-uniform. On 7,166 biopsy cores from 811 patients across seven centers under patient-level 5-fold cross-validation, the method reaches 67.1 macro AUC and 68.5 csPCa AUC, against 61.2 and 52.8 for the existing unpaired alignment method and 63.1 and 62.6 for the strongest unimodal baselines. Ablation against existing attention regularizers designed to prevent attention-uniformity collapse shows that such regularizers do not substitute for label-derived supervision: they constrain the attention distribution, whereas the signal required acts on the features that attention reads.

</details>


### [147] [PACE: Progressive Angular-to-Norm Contrastive Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15152)
*Yanping Li,Wei Zhou,Yawen Liu,Yibo Wang,Ke Zhu,Guangda Huzhang,Qing-Guo Chen,Zhao Xu,Jun Zhang,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PACE：先用余弦对比+低秩适配稳住角度几何，再切换点积相似+全参微调，用方向+范数共同编码语义，并配合“焦点嵌入损失”按置信度重加权难例；在多尺度主干与多模态检索任务上均优于基线且训练更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态嵌入多以余弦对比学习为主，训练稳定但只能利用角度信息，无法让范数承载语义；直接用更具表达力的点积相似会训练不稳定、效果反而差，原因在于过早扩展优化空间导致角-范数耦合、方向各向异性，并被全参微调加剧。

Method: 两阶段渐进式扩展表征与可训练参数空间：阶段I采用余弦相似目标+低秩适配（LoRA等）建立可靠的角度几何并限制优化自由度；阶段II切换为点积相似并开放全参微调，使方向与范数共同编码语义。另提出“焦点嵌入损失”，根据检索正样本置信度自适应重加权，下调容易样本、强调含竞争性负样本的歧义查询。

Result: 在不同规模骨干与多样多模态嵌入/检索基准上，PACE在准确率/检索质量上持续优于余弦对比基线与直接点积训练，并展现更稳定的训练动态。

Conclusion: 通过先稳角度几何再引入范数信号的渐进式训练，以及置信度自适应的损失设计，PACE有效解开角-范数耦合、缓解方向各向异性，使点积相似的表达力得到稳定释放，带来跨任务与规模的一致收益。

Abstract: Multimodal embedding models encode heterogeneous inputs into a shared embedding space, enabling efficient similarity computation across modalities and tasks. Most existing methods optimize cosine-based contrastive objectives, which promote stable training but restrict semantic compatibility to angular geometry, precluding embedding norms from serving as an additional semantic signal. However, directly optimizing the more expressive dot-product similarity, which leverages both angular and norm information, underperforms cosine-based training and exhibits unstable training dynamics. We attribute this discrepancy to premature optimization-space expansion, manifested as angular--norm entanglement and directional anisotropy in the representation space and further compounded by full-parameter fine-tuning. In this paper, we propose PACE, a two-stage framework that progressively expands both the representation and trainable parameter spaces. Stage I combines cosine-based objective with low-rank adaptation to establish a reliable angular geometry within constrained optimization spaces. Stage II switches to dot-product similarity and full-parameter fine-tuning, enabling embedding directions and norms to jointly encode semantic information. We further introduce Focal Embedding Loss, a confidence-adaptive objective that downweights queries with high positive retrieval confidence while emphasizing ambiguous queries with competitive negatives. Experiments across multiple backbone scales and diverse multimodal embedding tasks consistently validate the effectiveness of PACE.

</details>


### [148] [GRAVA: Grounded Reasoning-to-Action Representation and Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15169)
*Xiao Liu,Haoyu Li,Jianghao Leng,Lin Wang,Chao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAVA提出一种将“视觉-语言-动作”三要素在同一自回归流中统一的框架，通过可执行的规划接口把扎实落地的推理直接转译为可执行的连续轨迹，实现更强的物理证据对齐与驾驶表现提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽有中间“思考”，但常与物理场景证据脱节、与可执行动作接口松散，导致关键对象遵循差、闭环驾驶表现不足。作者希望把场景证据、因果推理与动作生成紧密绑定，实现从“看-想-做”的一致性与可执行性。

Method: 提出Grounded Reasoning-to-Action（GRA）：在单一自回归序列中同时进行 grounding、推理与动作生成。核心做法包括：1) 将与动作相关的语言指代绑定到2D视觉区域与自车物理状态；2) 用以轨迹为锚的类型化图组织对象交互与决策；3) 将该结构序列化为“有根基的推理”文本；4) 用单一VLM先生成推理，再输出精炼的“Executable Planner”动作，确定性解码为连续轨迹。数据与训练上：构建代理式GRA数据管线，结合前向场景grounding与后向轨迹锚定，生成GR-NavSim数据集（220万QA、7万GRA推理轨迹）；采用分阶段训练：预训练学扎实grounding与认知，模仿学习建立推理-动作接口，随后通过强化学习和探索优化驾驶行为。

Result: 在仅使用约60%人工驾驶示范进行动作监督的条件下，GRAVA-8B在NAVSIM全基准上取得纯自回归驾驶模型SOTA。在内部长尾评测上，相比仅预测动作的基线，引入完整GRA使关键目标合规率与闭环驾驶得分分别提升19.3%与20.5%。

Conclusion: 把与动作相关的物理证据在推理阶段保留下来并直达可执行动作，可显著提升闭环驾驶与对关键对象的遵循。GRA作为统一的落地推理到动作接口，为端到端驾驶提供了更稳健、可解释且数据高效的途径。

Abstract: Driving vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly reason before acting, but their intermediate reasoning is often weakly grounded in physical scene evidence and loosely connected to executable behavior. We present GRAVA, a framework built around Grounded Reasoning-to-Action (GRA), which unifies grounding, reasoning, and action generation in a single autoregressive stream. GRA links action-relevant language references to 2D visual regions and ego-centric physical states, organizes object interactions and decisions in a trajectory-anchored typed graph, and serializes this structure into grounded reasoning. A single VLM generates this reasoning followed by a compact Executable Planner action that is deterministically decoded into a continuous trajectory. We further introduce an agentic GRA data construction pipeline that combines forward scene grounding with backward trajectory anchoring, and use it to build GR-NavSim with 2.2M grounded question-answer pairs and 70K GRA reasoning traces. A progressive training strategy develops grounded cognition through pre-training, establishes the reasoning-to-action interface through imitation, and improves driving behavior through reinforcement learning and exploration. Using about 60% of the available human driving demonstrations for action supervision, GRAVA-8B achieves state-of-the-art performance among purely autoregressive driving models on the full NAVSIM benchmark. On an internal long-tail benchmark, full GRA improves key-object compliance and Closed-loop Driving Score by 19.3% and 20.5% over action-only prediction, respectively. These results show the benefit of preserving action-relevant physical evidence from grounded reasoning through executable action generation.

</details>


### [149] [EECTracker: Swarm Motion Prior-Guided Feature Compensation for Airborne Optical UAV Swarm Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15171)
*Zhaochen Chu,Tao Song,Ren Jin,Mingdong Jia,Defu Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EECTracker：利用群体运动先验和能量—熵一致性激活，在无人机编队空载光学场景中实现稳健的联检联跟，提高MOTA与IDF1并保持在线速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中光学UAV编队跟踪目标极小、视角变化快、背景杂乱，导致检测器响应间歇或缺失，传统多目标跟踪依赖稳定检测易出现轨迹碎片化与ID切换，需在检测不稳定时仍能稳态更新与关联。

Method: 1) 从可靠历史短轨构建概率化群体运动先验，刻画编队在像平面的共享短期运动趋势及不确定性；2) 基于该先验提出能量—熵一致性激活（EEC Activation），用特征残差能量与局部残差熵评估先验条件下的特征一致性；3) 计算局部EEC分数，引导像素级特征补偿，增强与先验一致的潜在目标响应、抑制背景；4) 将先验引导的补偿融入联检测-跟踪框架实现在线推理。

Result: 在AIRMOT与UAVSwarm数据集上优于SOTA；相对SCT-MOT，MOTA/IDF1分别提升：AIRMOT +3.89/+1.79 个百分点，UAVSwarm +2.81/+1.74 个百分点，并保持在线速度。

Conclusion: 群体运动先验结合能量—熵一致性度量，可在检测不稳定场景下有效补偿特征并稳健维护多目标状态与身份，实现对UAV编队的更强整体跟踪性能与实时性。

Abstract: Airborne optical tracking of uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms is challenging due to extremely small target scales, rapid viewpoint changes, and cluttered backgrounds, which can weaken target feature responses and lead to intermittent or temporarily missing detector responses. Existing multi-object tracking methods generally depend on reliable target-specific detector responses to maintain target states and identities across frames. When such responses become unreliable, target states cannot be reliably updated and cross-frame association cues become ambiguous, resulting in fragmented trajectories and identity switches. To address this problem, we propose EECTracker, a swarm-motion-prior-guided joint detection-and-tracking framework for airborne optical UAV swarm tracking. EECTracker constructs a probabilistic swarm motion prior from reliable historical tracklets to capture the shared short-term image-plane motion tendency of the swarm and its uncertainty, providing spatial guidance for cross-frame feature compensation. Building on this prior, we introduce Energy--Entropy Consistency Activation (EEC Activation) to evaluate motion-prior-conditioned feature consistency using feature residual energy and local residual entropy. The resulting Local EEC score guides pixel-level feature compensation by enhancing motion-prior-consistent feature responses in potential target regions while suppressing inconsistent background responses. Experiments on AIRMOT and UAVSwarm show that EECTracker achieves superior overall tracking performance compared with state-of-the-art methods. Compared with the strongest competing method SCT-MOT, EECTracker improves MOTA/IDF1 by 3.89/1.79 percentage points on AIRMOT and by 2.81/1.74 percentage points on UAVSwarm, while maintaining online inference speed.

</details>


### [150] [Does Attention-Guided Masking Really Help Object Discovery in Object-Centric Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15187)
*Youliang Tao,Yanhua Han,Bin Zhao,Juho Kannala,Joni Pajarinen,Rongzhen Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于注意力引导的遮挡(AGM)替代随机遮挡(RM)来做无监督对象中心学习中的自监督，但整体并不稳定：在真实纹理数据集上改善背景分割，前景发现不增反降或持平。结论：用内部注意力语义来改进掩码解码是有风险的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流OCL用Slot Attention汇聚特征并通过随机遮挡重建实现自监督。已有工作DIAS显示简单均匀随机遮挡也很强。作者怀疑：既然聚合阶段的注意力本身具备对象感知能力，能否利用其引导遮挡策略，从而得到更强的自监督信号？

Method: 在Slot Attention框架中，将聚合时的注意力权重用于指导图像patch的遮挡，形成Attention Guided Masking(AGM)，与随机遮挡(RM/DIAS风格)对比。考察两种槽初始化策略：无条件与条件初始化；在六个标准数据集上评估前景与背景分割/对象发现表现。

Result: AGM并非全面优于RM。在无条件初始化下，AGM在COCO与VOC等具真实纹理的数据集上显著提升背景分割；但无论条件或无条件初始化、跨多数据集，前景对象发现与RM相当或更差。

Conclusion: 内部注意力语义并不能稳定提升基于掩码重建的OCL；用注意力引导遮挡带来选择性收益（背景更好）却可能损伤前景发现。因此，基于注意力语义改造遮挡策略存在风险。源码与模型将开源。

Abstract: Object-Centric Learning (OCL) aims to decompose images into objects without human annotations. A major family of mainstream methods uses Slot Attention to aggregate image features into object-level representations and then from them reconstructs masked image content, i.e., Random Masking (RM), to provide self-supervision. The recent method DIAS simply masks image patches at uniform randomness yet achieves competitive object discovery accuracy. Since attention during aggregation already possesses object discovery ability, we explore using it to develop a better image patch masking strategy, i.e., Attention Guided Masking (AGM), thereby providing better self-supervision. Results on six recognized datasets show that AGM does not always outperform RM. Under unconditional slot initialization, AGM substantially improves background segmentation on datasets with realistic textures (COCO and VOC); Regardless of conditional or unconditional slot initialization and across datasets, foreground object discovery remains comparable or decreases. We suggest peer researchers in the OCL community that attempts to exploit internal attention semantics to improve OCL with masked decoding are risky. Our source code, model checkpoints and evaluation logs will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [151] [Reconstructing Is Not Acting: Action-Centric Latent Dynamics Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15189)
*Dingjie Fu,Dianxing Shi,Yangyang Xu,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ACT-LAM以解决重建型潜在动作模型中的“重建-动作不匹配”，通过更强的动作提取与利用，在VP^2等基准上以更少参数和更低计算量取得显著提升（平均成功率+7.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LAM依赖重建未来状态，但更低的重建误差未必带来更好的潜在动作表示与下游性能。原因在于：1）逆动力学模型未被显式鼓励从与动作相关的转变中去除外观干扰；2）前向动力学模型可绕过潜在动作，利用当前状态的捷径进行预测，导致潜在动作利用不足。

Method: 提出ACT-LAM，聚焦动作中心化的表征学习并精简特征处理以将容量集中于潜在动力学建模。关键组件：1）AQ-IDM：使用可学习的action queries与门控聚合，从状态转变中选择性提取富含动作线索的表征，避免强信息瓶颈；2）AT-FDM：将潜在动作映射为action tokens，与随时间演化的状态表示逐步交互，实现持续、具状态感知的动作条件化；并减少冗余处理降低计算与参数规模。

Result: 在多个机器人数据集与VP^2基准上取得更强的潜在动作一致性、前向动力学质量与下游视觉规划成功率，且参数更少、计算开销更低；在VP^2聚合成功率上较SOTA提升7.6%。

Conclusion: 仅靠重建目标会导致潜在动作学习的错配。通过强化动作提取与利用的ACT-LAM，可在降低资源消耗的同时显著提升潜在动作质量与视觉规划性能，证明动作中心化设计对无监督动作学习至关重要。

Abstract: Latent action models (LAMs) learn action representations from unlabeled videos by inferring latent actions from visual transitions and reconstructing future states. However, we identify a fundamental $\textbf{reconstruction-action mismatch}$: lower reconstruction error does not necessarily yield better latent dynamics or downstream performance. We attribute this mismatch to two underconstrained aspects of reconstruction-based latent dynamics modeling: (i) the inverse dynamics model (IDM) is not explicitly encouraged to distinguish action-related transitions from nuisance appearance, and (ii) the forward dynamics model (FDM) can underutilize the inferred latent action by exploiting predictive shortcuts from the current state. To address both limitations, we propose $\textbf{ACT-LAM}$, a lightweight action-centric framework that strengthens both action extraction and action utilization. Specifically, its Action Query IDM (AQ-IDM) employs learnable action queries and gated aggregation to selectively extract rich action-related transition cues without strong information bottlenecks. And its Action Token FDM (AT-FDM) projects latent actions into action tokens that progressively interact with evolving state representations, enabling continuous state-aware action conditioning. ACT-LAM further streamlines feature processing to concentrate model capacity on latent dynamics modeling. Extensive experiments on several robotic datasets and the VP$^2$ benchmark demonstrate stronger latent action consistency, forward dynamics, and downstream visual planning performance with fewer trainable parameters and lower computational overhead. In particular, ACT-LAM surpasses the previous state of the art by $\textbf{7.6%}$ on the aggregated VP$^2$ success rate. Codes at $\href{https://github.com/DingjieFu/ACT-LAM}{url}$.

</details>


### [152] [Long-to-Short Video Evidence Reasoning for Grounded Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15224)
*Kaiyan Chen,Junbin Xiao,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LOVER 是一种用于视频指证式问答（GQA）的“长到短”证据强化模型，通过课程化强化学习从长时段取证逐步适配到短时推理，并在开源模型中达成SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的视频推理方法在长视频取证与短期精细推理间存在迁移困难，且证据定位奖励与时间可观察性不足，导致问答与指证精度受限。

Method: 提出三项与模型无关且互补的设计：1) 长到短的视频证据课程学习：按证据时长组织RL训练，逐步从长范围定位过渡到短期推理；2) GQA奖励：采用IoP（与预测证据交叠的比例）替代传统IoU，更契合“证据命中”而非严格时间段重叠；3) 自适应时间戳渲染：基于背景自适应地在帧上渲染时间戳的位置与颜色，增强时间信息可见性。

Result: 在多种骨干上均提升问答、取证与指证式问答表现；基于 Time-R1 的 LOVER 在 NExT-GQA 与 ReXTime 等主流基准上取得开源模型的最新SOTA；消融实验验证三项组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过课程化RL训练、面向证据命中的奖励设计与增强时间可见性的渲染策略，LOVER 有效缩小长视频取证与短期推理间的鸿沟，全面提升GQA性能，具备通用可迁移性。

Abstract: We present LOVER, a \underline{L}ong to sh\underline{O}rt \underline{V}ideo \underline{E}vidence \underline{R}einforced model for grounded question answering (GQA). LOVER highlights three innovations over existing reinforcement-learning (RL) based video reasoning models: (1) \textbf{Long-to-short Video Evidence Curriculum Learning}, which organizes RL training according to evidence duration and progressively adapts the model from long-range grounding to short-term reasoning; (2) \textbf{GQA Rewards}, which underscore the benefit of IoP reward over IoU for evidence spotting rather than strict temporal span overlap; (3) \textbf{Adaptive Timestamp Rendering}, which adaptively renders timestamps onto video frames using background-aware position and color selection to enhance temporal observability. The three designs are model-agnostic and reciprocal. They effectively improve QA, grounding, and grounded QA performance over different backbones. Notably, LOVER built on Time-R1 achieves new state-of-the-art (SOTA) results among open-source models on popular GQA benchmarks: NExT-GQA and ReXTime. Comprehensive ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of our three innovative components.

</details>


### [153] [Deep Learning-based Intelligent Diagnosis of Congenital Uterine Anomalies in 3D Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15225)
*Yueyue Xu,Yuhao Huang,Jiaxiao Deng,Yuanji Zhang,Haoming Zhang,Jiajia Qu,Shiying Zheng,Xiaomei Tang,Haining Chen,Chengcai Chen,Yiyi Wu,Xin Yang,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CUA-Net，一种无需冠状面重建即可自动分类先天性子宫畸形的3D超声AI模型；在内外部测试中取得>91%准确率和接近1.0的AUC，优于初级医师、接近高级医师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA诊断依赖冠状面重建与专家经验，流程繁琐、易受操作者影响，且类别不均衡、少见畸形识别困难；需要一个对临床流程友好、泛化性强的自动化方法。

Method: 基于3D ResNet-18构建CUA-Net；引入动态数据重采样缓解类别不平衡；困难样本挖掘通过损失加权强化学习难例；自监督重建以更全面地学习体数据表示；在线数据增强用于纠正错误预测并提升泛化；与多种深度学习方法及不同资历超声医师对比；评价指标含准确率、精确率、召回率、F1、micro-/macro-AUC。

Result: 内部测试集：准确率93.88%，精确率87.01%，召回95.92%，F1 88.09%，micro-AUC 0.9982，macro-AUC 0.9997；外部测试集：准确率91.52%，精确率83.27%，召回88.63%，F1 81.49%，micro-AUC 0.9945，macro-AUC 0.9990。整体优于初级医师，多数指标接近高级医师。

Conclusion: CUA-Net在常见CUA分类上准确、具良好泛化，对少见畸形有初步识别潜力；有望优化临床流程并促进诊断标准化。

Abstract: Objective: To develop an intelligent framework, termed CUA-Net, for the automated classification of congenital uterine anomalies (CUA) without requiring coronal plane reconstruction, and to evaluate its clinical applicability.
  Methods: CUA-Net was built on 3D ResNet-18, equipped with a dynamic data resampling strategy to mitigate the data imbalance issue and a hard sample mining technique to fully learn from the difficult cases by loss adjustment. We further proposed the self-supervised reconstruction to comprehensively explore the volumes and the online data augmentation to refine the wrong predictions and enhance the model's generalization. We compared the CUA-Net with different deep-learning methods and junior/senior sonographers in the testing set. The evaluation metrics included accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, micro-AUC, and macro-AUC.
  Results: The proposed CUA-Net exhibited satisfactory performance in both internal and external test sets. In the internal cohort, the model achieved accuracy of 93.88%, precision of 87.01%, recall of 95.92%, F1-score of 88.09%, and micro-AUC of 0.9982 and macro-AUC of 0.9997. In the external set, it maintained good performance with accuracy of 91.52%, precision of 83.27%, recall of 88.63%, F1-score of 81.49%, micro-AUC of 0.9945 and macro-AUC of 0.9990. Our CUA-Net outperformed the junior sonographers across all performance indicators and achieved performance comparable to that of the senior sonographers across most metrics.
  Conclusion: The CUA-Net demonstrates favorable accuracy and generalizability in classifying common CUA categories, while showing preliminary potential for recognizing less prevalent anomalies. These capabilities may help optimize clinical workflows and support more standardized diagnosis.

</details>


### [154] [Closed-form Bayesian homography estimation from noisy point correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15227)
*Hanne Beuter,Sebastian Dorn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种快速贝叶斯单应估计方法，显式建模观测噪声与先验，并输出单应参数的后验分布；给出齐次坐标下后验均值闭式解并用迭代贝叶斯处理非线性，在合成与拼接实验中较DLT更准且能给出不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单应估计多给点估计，不直接量化由噪声带来的不确定性；然而不确定性会传递到标定、三维重建等后续步骤，且在医疗、自动驾驶、国防等安全关键场景尤为重要，因此需要能同时融合测量不确定性与先验并输出后验的不确定性建模方法。

Method: 构建基于点对应的贝叶斯单应估计：把观测噪声与先验纳入概率模型，推导齐次坐标下单应后验均值的闭式解；为应对模型中的非线性项，提出迭代的贝叶斯更新（近似/线性化-更新循环）以逼近后验；最终得到单应参数的完整后验分布，可用于不确定性量化。

Result: 在合成数据的投影变换场景下，相比DLT，在不同噪声水平下取得更低的估计误差；在真实图像拼接上，方法能生成可用的单应并同时输出不确定性信息，展示了对真实对应点的适用性。

Conclusion: 该方法在速度与精度间取得良好折中，能显式输出单应估计的不确定性；对于需不确定性传播的视觉任务（如标定、重建、拼接）更可靠，优于仅给点估计的传统DLT。

Abstract: While homographies are fundamental to many computer vision tasks, the majority of conventional estimation techniques provide only point estimates without directly quantifying uncertainty introduced by noisy observations. Uncertainty, though, propagates to subsequent processing steps such as camera calibration and 3D reconstruction and is particularly relevant in safety-critical and socially relevant fields including medical imaging, autonomous driving, and defense. We present a fast Bayesian formulation for homography estimation from point correspondences that explicitly incorporates measurement uncertainty and prior knowledge while providing a posterior distribution over the homography parameters. A closed-form solution of the posterior mean of the homography is derived in homogeneous coordinates and supplemented by an iterative Bayesian approach to handle non-linearities. Synthetic experiments demonstrate the applicability to projective transformations and show improved estimation accuracy over DLT under varying noise conditions. Image stitching experiments further demonstrate applicability to real image correspondences while additionally providing uncertainty information.

</details>


### [155] [Unsupervised Point Cloud Registration via Training-Time Semantic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15228)
*Kezheng Xiong,Shiyun Xu,Sheng Ao,Siqi Shen,Cheng Wang,Chenglu Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAESAR，一个在训练期借助现成3D语义分割教师的无监督LiDAR点云配准框架，通过语义引导的重匹配、伪标签挖掘与语义蒸馏，显著提升KITTI与nuScenes上的配准精度，推理零额外开销、无需语义标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外大规模LiDAR点云无监督配准受几何歧义与稀疏低分辨扫描影响，伪标签质量差、收敛不稳。作者发现配准模型天然具有与精度强相关的“语义感知”，但在无监督噪声监督下易瓦解，导致性能崩塌，亟需在不增加推理成本和标注需求的前提下稳固这种语义结构。

Method: 提出CAESAR教师-学生框架，仅在训练期使用现成3D分割模型提供语义先验：1) Dual-Cue Guided Re-Matching：利用语义与几何双线索对候选匹配进行“重选而非拒绝”，从噪声特征空间中找回被少量伪近邻掩盖的内点匹配；2) Semantic-Geometric Label Mining：对教师进行轻量、批次级细化，在语义引导下挖掘更可靠的伪标签；3) Semantic Predictive Distillation：在特征空间蒸馏并巩固学生的语义感知。整个语义引导仅用于训练，推理阶段无额外模块。

Result: 在KITTI与nuScenes上达到SOTA，尤其在难度更高、点更稀疏的nuScenes上有显著提升；验证了语义感知与配准精度的强相关性以及所提三项技术对鲁棒性与收敛的改善。

Conclusion: 通过在训练期引入外部语义教师并设计重匹配、伪标签挖掘与语义蒸馏三管齐下，CAESAR在不需语义标注且推理零开销的前提下，有效缓解无监督配准中的几何歧义与伪标签噪声问题，稳固并利用模型内生语义，从而带来显著性能提升。

Abstract: Unsupervised registration of large-scale LiDAR point clouds remains challenging due to the geometric ambiguity inherent in outdoor scenes, which degrades pseudo-label quality and leads to suboptimal convergence, particularly for sparse, low-resolution scans such as those from nuScenes. We reveal that registration models intrinsically encode semantic awareness that strongly correlates with registration accuracy, albeit without explicit semantic supervision. However, this native awareness is fragile: noisy supervision arising from geometric ambiguity in unsupervised settings rapidly erodes the learned semantic structure, causing performance collapse. To this end, we propose CAESAR, a teacher-student framework guided by an off-the-shelf 3D segmentation model exclusively during training. We observe that potential inlier matches are often buried just beneath a few spurious neighbors in the noisy feature space, motivating Dual-Cue Guided Re-Matching to recover them through reselection rather than simply rejecting. Building on this, a train-only Semantic-Geometric Label Mining performs lightweight, batch-specific teacher refinement and mines reliable pseudo-labels under semantic guidance. We further introduce Semantic Predictive Distillation to consolidate the student's semantic awareness in the feature space. Extensive experiments on KITTI and nuScenes demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, with pronounced gains on the challenging nuScenes benchmark. Crucially, CAESAR incurs zero inference overhead and requires no semantic annotations on the registration data. Code will be released.

</details>


### [156] [Pre-PEFT Probing: Weight Statistics and Perturbation Robustness for Layer Selection in VLM Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15229)
*Qingtao Xia,Jiahua Bao,Siyao Cheng,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种在微调前用于选择PEFT层的探测方法，通过统计权重与扰动鲁棒性来预测哪些视觉编码器层最“可适配”，以在更少可训练参数下获得更稳定、更高的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有做法常对所有层一刀切地插入LoRA等适配器，层选择多凭经验，导致参数效率不高、收益不稳定。需要在微调前判定哪些层最值得适配，以降低可训练参数同时维持或提升性能。

Method: 聚焦VLM的视觉编码器Transformer层，对每层进行两类指标评估：(1) Q/K/V投影权重的统计性质（如范数、条件数）；(2) 在受控参数扰动下的鲁棒性。将这些“可适配性”指标与在仅对单层应用不同PEFT（5种变体）时的下游性能增益进行系统对比与相关性分析，覆盖7个基准。

Result: 跨7个基准与5种PEFT，一致观察到：权重范数更大、条件数更高的层（或矩阵）对扰动更鲁棒，且更可能带来更大的微调增益。指标与增益呈稳定相关，为层选择提供了可操作信号。

Conclusion: 在微调前进行分布统计与扰动测试可有效指导适配层选择，在减少可训练参数的同时保持或提升性能，为VLM的参数高效自适应提供实用流程。

Abstract: We propose a pre-fine-tuning probing method for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) layer selection, aiming to obtain more stable and higher gains with fewer trainable parameters when adapting large vision--language models (VLMs). Unlike the common practice of applying LoRA and other adapters to all layers at once---where layer selection often relies on heuristic rules---we focus on the vision encoder and directly evaluate the "adaptability'' of each Transformer layer. Specifically, we characterize each layer from two perspectives: (i) the statistical properties of its Q/K/V projection weights (e.g., norms and condition numbers); (ii) robustness under controlled parameter perturbations. We then systematically compare these indicators with the downstream performance gains brought by applying PEFT to a single layer. Across experiments covering seven benchmarks and five PEFT variants, we observe a consistent correlation: layers (or matrices) with larger weight norms and higher condition numbers are usually more robust to perturbations and are more likely to yield larger fine-tuning gains. These results show that distribution-statistics analysis and perturbation tests before fine-tuning can provide practical signals for adaptation-layer selection, thereby maintaining or improving performance while reducing trainable parameters.

</details>


### [157] [A 25-$μ$s/inf Event-driven Graph Neural Network Processor with Spatiotemporal Caching and Spline Convolution for Ultra-low-latency AI at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15241)
*Adrian Kneip,Martin Lefebvre,Daniel Gehrig,Victoria Catalán Pastor,Davide Scaramuzza,Marian Verhelst,Charlotte Frenkel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ETHEREAL 是首个面向事件相机 EV-GNN 的高分辨率加速器，通过邻居并行的样条卷积与2D/3D分裂内存层级及ROI时空缓存，实现640×480规模、25.6μs端到端延迟、1.7μJ/事件能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机产生微秒级、稀疏且异步的事件流，传统帧式视觉与常规加速器难以高效处理。EV-GNN 算法虽适配事件数据，但其计算呈现“计算致密 + 访存稀疏不规则”并存，给硬件带来新的并行与带宽/缓存挑战，需要专用的算法-硬件协同设计。

Method: 提出 ETHEREAL 加速器：1) 邻居并行的样条卷积引擎，匹配EV-GNN中的图卷积/样条核特性以并行化邻域聚合；2) 2D/3D 分裂内存层级，分别优化图拓扑/特征与时空体素表示的存取；3) 基于兴趣区域(ROI)的时空缓存机制，利用事件流时空局部性减少稀疏不规则访存；整体面向640×480分辨率可扩展。

Result: 在当前最先进任务/模型上，实现端到端推理延迟25.6μs、单位事件能耗1.7μJ；支持640×480分辨率的事件流处理。

Conclusion: 通过面向EV-GNN的专用并行算子与分层缓存/存储设计，ETHEREAL 在高分辨率事件视觉上实现超低时延与高能效，验证了算法-硬件协同应对稀疏不规则访存与致密计算并存挑战的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic-vision-sensor (DVS) cameras generate events on a per-pixel basis with a $μ$s-level temporal resolution, calling for new algorithm-hardware co-design approaches compared to standard frame-based vision. While event-driven graph neural networks (EV-GNNs) emerge as a promising algorithmic solution, they raise new HW challenges by mixing dense-regular compute operations and sparse-irregular memory accesses. We present ETHEREAL, the first EV-GNN accelerator that scales to 640$\times$480 resolutions, thanks to a neighbor-parallel spline convolution engine and a 2D/3D-split memory hierarchy with a novel region-of-interest spatiotemporal caching mechanism. Measurement results demonstrate end-to-end inference with 25.6$μ$s latency and 1.7$μ$J energy per event on state-of-the-art workloads

</details>


### [158] [AlignUS: MRI-Guided Ultrasound Representation Learning for ALS Classification from Tongue Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15285)
*Kadija Abdel Ghader,Emani Babe,Lorenzo Pettinari,Meya Haroune,Sidaty El Hadramy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AlignUS：一种将MRI解剖知识蒸馏到高分辨率舌部超声（HRUS）分类器的跨模态框架，仅用HRUS推理即可显著提升ALS（延髓受累）评估，四折患者级验证达BA 0.958、Macro-F1 0.963、ROC-AUC 0.990。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALS早期评估困难，尤其在MRI缺乏的资源受限环境。HRUS便携低成本，但数据量小且单模态表征鲁棒性差，限制了可靠诊断模型的学习。需要一种方法在仅用HRUS推理的同时，利用MRI蕴含的解剖信息提升HRUS模型性能。

Method: 提出AlignUS跨模态知识蒸馏框架：训练阶段同时使用MRI与HRUS，对齐两者表征；损失函数包含三部分——分类损失（监督诊断）、有监督对比学习（拉近同类、分离异类，增强判别性与鲁棒性）、特征级蒸馏（最小化HRUS特征与MRI嵌入的差异）。推理阶段仅需HRUS输入。

Result: 在四折患者级交叉验证下，聚合指标：平衡准确率0.958、宏F1 0.963、ROC-AUC 0.990；相较HRUS基线与其他跨模态方法均有一致提升。

Conclusion: MRI来源的监督可显著改进基于超声的ALS评估，同时在部署时保持低成本与对MRI的独立性；跨模态蒸馏有效弥合资源受限场景下的成像差距。

Abstract: Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS) is a progressive neurodegenerative disease in which early assessment remains challenging, particularly in low-resource settings where MRI is often unavailable. High-resolution ultrasound (HRUS) of the tongue offers a portable and low-cost alternative for evaluating bulbar involvement, but learning reliable diagnostic models is limited by small datasets and the difficulty of extracting robust representations from ultrasound alone. We propose AlignUS, a cross-modal knowledge distillation framework that transfers anatomical knowledge from MRI to a HRUS-based classifier while requiring only HRUS at inference time. The model combines classification loss, supervised contrastive learning, and feature-level distillation to align HRUS representations with MRI embeddings. AlignUS achieves a patient-level balanced accuracy of 0.958, macro-F1 of 0.963, and ROC-AUC of 0.990, aggregated across four patient-level cross-validation folds, with consistent improvements over HRUS baselines and cross-modal alternatives. These results demonstrate that MRI-derived supervision can substantially improve ultrasound-based ALS assessment while preserving low-cost, inference-time independence from MRI.

</details>


### [159] [Learning from Reliable Negatives: Confidence-Anchored Test-Time Adaptation for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15307)
*Yizhou Liu,Fei Tang,Yuchen Yan,Zhengxi Lu,Songqin Nong,Tao Jiang,Wenhao Xu,Wenqi Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需标注的测试时训练范式，用坐标token置信度与负样本学习提升GUI定位；CANL-7B在ScreenSpot-V2达92.1%，在更难的ScreenSpot-Pro达33.8%，显著超基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI定位需要把自然语言映射到屏幕坐标，但监督/强化学习依赖昂贵标注，难以规模化。作者观察到：整体序列置信度不如坐标token置信度有效；在稀疏坐标空间里，负样本比噪声较大的正样本更可靠。

Method: 提出两步方法：1) Confidence-Anchored Learning (CAL)：以坐标token置信度筛选伪标签，并用与目标距离相关的二元奖励信号进行更新。2) Confidence-Anchored Negative Learning (CANL)：仅用负样本优化，利用坐标token置信度锚定“非目标”区域，规避错误正样本带来的风险；属于测试时无标注训练范式。

Result: 在ScreenSpot-V2上，CANL-7B达92.1%；在更具挑战的ScreenSpot-Pro上达33.8%，比基线提升8.9个百分点。表明坐标token置信度可作为强信号替代人工标注。

Conclusion: 坐标token级置信度与负样本驱动的测试时训练可在无人工标注下有效提升GUI grounding性能，提供可扩展的GUI智能体训练路径。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) grounding is essential for autonomous agents to map natural language instructions to precise screen coordinates. However, existing supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning methods are constrained by the high cost of annotation, creating a scalability bottleneck. In this paper, we introduce a label-free test-time training paradigm driven by two key insights: (1) confidence patterns in coordinate tokens are a better indicator than full-sequence confidence, and (2) in sparse GUI coordinate spaces, negative samples offer more reliable learning signals than potentially noisy positive ones. We first propose Confidence-Anchored Learning (CAL), which utilizes coordinate-token confidence to filter pseudo-labels and assign distance-based binary rewards. Building on this, we develop Confidence-Anchored Negative Learning (CANL), which exclusively optimizes the model using negative samples to bypass the risks of incorrect positive samples. Experimental results demonstrate that CANL-7B achieves 92.1% on ScreenSpot-V2. On more challenging ScreenSpot-Pro, CANL-7B reaches 33.8%, an 8.9% absolute improvement over the base model. Our findings establish coordinate-token confidence as a powerful alternative to manual annotations for scalable GUI agent development.

</details>


### [160] [Hypergraph-Regularized Gramian Volumes for Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15320)
*Anindya Nag,Ambuj Mehrish,Sebastiano Vascon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HyVol：在训练时用超图正则化改进体积式多模态检索的嵌入，对齐语义相近样本并保留推理成本与结构不变，显著提升零样本检索R@1，尤其在V2T上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统体积式多模态检索（视频、音频、字幕）能捕获候选内部高阶对齐，但评分是候选局部的；语义相近样本在对比学习中往往互为负样本，削弱了跨样本语义结构的利用效率。作者希望在不改变推理阶段成本与结构的前提下，于训练中显式引入跨样本语义关系，缓解“好样本互伤”的问题。

Method: 引入Hypergraph-Regularized Gramian Volumes（HyVol）作为训练期模块：
- 构建超图：文档超边连接同一候选的已观测模态；语义超边连接基于“分离的字幕嵌入”互为top-k近邻的候选。
- 采用浅层门控超图网络，对各模态嵌入进行残差校正；以存在掩码避免缺失模态的消息传递；用identity padding在不填充特征的前提下保持已观测Gram子矩阵的行列式不变。
- 在嵌入层面做细化而非分数层面，因此同样适用于Gram与HyperGram体积损失。
- 训练后移除超图模块，保留原骨干与打分函数，推理代价不变。

Result: 在VAST-27M的15万剪辑子集上训练两种骨干，零样本评估于六个基准。成对协议下（paired protocol），HyVol在五个检索基准的R@1全部提升：V2T在MSR-VTT上+8.3、VATEX上+7.6。缺失模态掩码（missing-modality masking）下，V2T增益在所有设置均为正；T2V在四个设置中略为负。

Conclusion: 在不增加推理负担的前提下，利用超图正则化注入跨样本语义结构，可稳健提升体积式多模态检索，尤其V2T方向显著；对缺失模态具有一定鲁棒性，但T2V在部分设置存在小幅回退。

Abstract: Volume-based multimodal retrieval jointly scores a text query with a candidate's video, audio, and subtitle embeddings. While this approach captures higher-order within-candidate alignment, the score remains candidate-local, and semantically related training samples primarily serve as contrastive negatives. This work introduces Hypergraph-Regularized Gramian Volumes (HyVol), a training-time module that incorporates these semantic relations prior to evaluating the original volume loss. Document hyperedges connect the observed modalities of each candidate, whereas semantic hyperedges link candidates whose detached captions are mutual top-k neighbors. A shallow gated hyper-graph network applies residual corrections to the modality embeddings. Presence masks exclude unavailable streams from message passing, and identity padding preserves the determinant of the observed Gram submatrix without feature imputation. As refinement operates on embeddings rather than scores, the same construction applies to both Gram and HyperGram. We remove the hypergraph after training, leaving the backbone-only architecture, original scoring function, and retrieval cost unchanged. We train both backbones on a 150K-clip subset of VAST-27M and evaluate zero-shot performance on six benchmarks. Under the paired protocol, HyVol improves R@1 across all five retrieval benchmarks, with video-to-text gains reaching +8.3 on MSR-VTT and +7.6 on VATEX. Under missing-modality masking, the V2T margin remains positive in all experimental settings, although the T2V margin becomes slightly negative in four.

</details>


### [161] [Concept-Grounded Reasoning with Prompt-Driven Localization for Interpretable Structured Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15334)
*Xinyue Xu,Hongbin Lin,Juangui Xu,Hualiang Wang,Lehan Wang,Lijie Hu,Weiyang Liu,Adrian Weller,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CORAL框架：在医学影像报告生成中引入病灶定位与概念瓶颈，联合文本概念与掩膜调制视觉特征，提升诊断准确性、概念一致性与报告质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型多采用端到端融合，缺少对临床定义的中间属性与空间证据的显式建模，导致可解释性与临床对齐不足。

Method: 构建CORAL：1) 用提示驱动的医学分割模型进行病灶空间定位；2) 通过Concept Bottleneck模块预测多类临床属性并转化为文本化概念token；3) 将概念token与由病灶掩膜调制的视觉特征一起输入MLLM，执行结构化报告生成与诊断预测。

Result: 在BUS-CoT与IU X-ray数据集上，相比强基线（通用与医学MLLM），在诊断准确度、概念一致性与报告质量上均取得稳定提升。

Conclusion: 将概念监督与空间定位纳入统一推理流程能更好地对齐临床决策过程，提升医学报告生成的可解释性与可靠性。

Abstract: Medical imaging modalities such as ultrasound and X-ray are widely used in clinical practice, where diagnosis follows a structured, evidence-driven workflow aligned with standardized criteria. While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) show promise for automated medical report generation, most existing systems rely on end-to-end multimodal fusion without modeling clinically defined intermediate attributes, leading to limited grounding and interpretability. To address this issue, we propose CORAL (COncept-grounded ReAsoning with Localization), a multimodal framework that integrates spatial grounding and concept-level supervision into a unified reasoning process. CORAL employs a prompt-driven medical segmentation model to localize lesions and predicts multi-class clinical attributes through a Concept Bottleneck module. The resulting textual concept tokens are combined with mask-modulated visual features within an MLLM to enable structured report generation and diagnostic prediction. Experiments on BUS-CoT and IU X-ray datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in diagnostic accuracy, concept consistency, and report quality over strong general-purpose and medical MLLMs, indicating that concept-grounded reasoning better aligns generation with clinical decision processes.

</details>


### [162] [Query-Conditioned Spherical Centroid Aggregation for Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15335)
*Ambuj Mehrish,Anindya Nag,Sebastiano Vascon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SCALAR，一种按查询自适应加权的球面质心聚合器，解决多模态检索中对称聚合常弱于最佳单模态的问题，在多基准上带来稳定正增益，并在模态缺失下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何聚合（Gramian体积、双曲体积、谱目标）对所有模态一视同仁，导致聚合后的检索准确率常低于最强单模态通道；分析表明根因是模态影响被均匀化。需要一种能根据查询与可用模态相关性动态分配权重的聚合方法，适配不完整模态并在统一评测下提升R@1。

Method: 提出SCALAR：1) 查询条件的相关性预测，为每个可用模态学习自适应权重；2) 在单位球面上计算加权质心作为联合表示（球面几何保证方向性与尺度稳健）；3) 通过对模态子集的掩码/降元训练以适配任意模态缺失；4) 使用rank-8 LoRA适配，参数仅4.8M；5) 统一协议评测，并包含均匀权重消融与与GRAM等方法对比。

Result: 在五个基准中四个实现正的聚合增益，最高+4.0 R@1；无先前对称聚合器能在超过一个基准上为正。均匀权重消融复现性能下降。文本到视频R@1在三个基准上最高、在第四个与最优在随机种子波动范围内。测试时模态dropout下，表示阶段比分发的GRAM检查点在所有遮蔽率与基准上高3.2–10.9 R@1。

Conclusion: 对称聚合会稀释强模态信号；SCALAR通过查询条件的相关性加权与球面质心聚合，稳健提升多模态检索，尤其在模态缺失时优势明显。但两阶段检索中的重排序器若只在完整模态上训练，会在缺失模态时退化为视频通道，削弱表示层收益，提示需要在重排序阶段同样考虑模态缺失与相关性建模。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval integrates video, audio, subtitles, and text; however, recent geometric aggregators, such as Gramian volumes, hyperbolic volumes, and spectral objectives, treat all modalities symmetrically. Under a unified evaluation protocol, their joint scores frequently lag behind the strongest single-modality pathway by 1.9 to 27.6 R@1. Controlled analyses attribute this outcome to uniform modality influence. This work introduces Spherical Centroid Aggregation with Learned Adaptive Relevance (SCALAR), a query-conditioned aggregator that assigns relevance-based weights to each available modality before computing a spherical centroid. SCALAR accommodates arbitrary modality subsets and is trained on masked, reduced-arity views using rank-8 LoRA adapters. Across five benchmarks, SCALAR achieves positive aggregation gain on four, reaching +4.0 R@1, while none of the evaluated prior aggregators is positive on more than one. A uniform-weight ablation reproduces the degradation observed with symmetric aggregation. With only 4.8 million trainable parameters, SCALAR attains the highest text-to-video R@1 on three and performs within seed variation of the best result on a fourth. Under test-time modality dropout, SCALAR's representation-stage score surpasses the released GRAM checkpoint at every evaluated masking rate and benchmark by 3.2 to 10.9 R@1. Finally, as modalities are removed, rerankers trained exclusively on complete modality sets increasingly converge toward their video-only pathways, diminishing these representation-level gains and underscoring a limitation of standard two-stage retrieval pipelines.

</details>


### [163] [Robust Multi-Model Fitting through Learning Neighbor Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15348)
*Chang Nie,Guangming Wang,Zhe Liu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LNR粗到细框架，用神经网络先筛优质最小集再用邻域区域特征细化与评分，无需对采样与求解器求导，在四个多模型拟合任务上达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模型拟合常受四类瓶颈限制：特征利用不足、优化低效、模型重叠导致歧义、以及端到端不可微，影响在噪声与多结构场景（如重建/混合现实）中的鲁棒性与效率。

Method: 采用两级框架：1) 粗级：对最小集的局部点对关系与全局上下文进行神经特征提取，输出置信度预筛少量优质最小集，减少坏最小集带来的计算浪费；2) 细级：为每个假设编码可共享的邻域区域几何特征，网络对每个假设独立细化并打分。训练信号直接来自数据点特征而非假设参数，从而绕开对随机采样与模型求解器的求导。

Result: 在四个经典多模型拟合任务上取得SOTA，验证了鲁棒性、精度与效率的提升（更少计算代价、更好处理模型重叠）。

Conclusion: LNR通过“先筛再精”的邻域区域特征学习兼顾效率与准确度，避免不可微模块带来的训练障碍，具有良好泛化性，易于扩展到各类鲁棒多模型拟合任务。

Abstract: Multi-model fitting involves fitting multiple models accurately in a noisy environment. It is the basis for computer vision tasks such as scene reconstruction and mixed reality. However, its performance is often limited by insufficient feature utilization, inefficient optimization, model overlap, and the non-differentiable pipelines. To overcome these limitations, we introduce a robust coarse-to-fine framework called Learning Neighbor Regions (LNR). Recognizing that substantial computational resources are wasted on numerous bad minimum sets, we propose the coarse-level module. This module utilizes a neural network to extract and analyze geometric feature of both local point-wise relationships and global contextual information in minimum sets, outputting confidence to pre-select a small number of good minimum sets, thereby enhancing overall efficiency before solving hypotheses. To address model overlap, LNR encodes neighbor region features for each hypothesis in its fine-level module. These region features consist of geometric features of neighboring data points, which can be used by multiple regions simultaneously. This design allows the neural network to individually refine and score each hypothesis. Importantly, LNR is trained to learn directly from data point features rather than from the hypothesis parameters, thus avoiding differentiating the sampling process and the model solvers. Extensive experiments on four classic multi-model fitting tasks demonstrate that LNR achieves state-of-the-art performance. The analysis suggests that LNR can be easily adapted to various robust multi-model fitting tasks.

</details>


### [164] [End-to-End Cell Detection via Instance-aware Graph Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15354)
*Ruochen Liu,Yalin Zheng,Jingxin Liu,Jianfeng Zhang,Shoujun Huang,Dexing Kong,Haofeng Li,Wei Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个端到端的细胞检测与分类框架，动态构建实例图并在实例感知图网络中融合外观与关系信息，相比两阶段方法在多数据集上显著提升检测与分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的方法多为“视觉特征提取→关系建模”的两阶段流程，需分别调参，难以端到端联合优化，且可能不足以捕获肿瘤微环境中复杂的细胞交互。

Method: 以图像补丁级特征为基础，使用可学习查询生成细胞实例表示，并通过融合特征相似度与空间距离动态构图；在此之上引入实例感知图网络，执行自适应实例筛选与特征重组，将节点聚合为拓扑潜在状态，并通过由视觉线索驱动的选择性状态空间转移机制，实现外观与关系证据的融合；整体端到端训练。

Result: 在多种染色协议、多个细胞/细胞核检测数据集上，相比现有方法在检测与分类指标上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 端到端的动态构图与实例感知图网络能够有效联合建模外观与关系信息，降低流水线复杂度并提升跨数据集的检测与分类效果；代码将开源，具有可复现性与推广潜力。

Abstract: Accurate cell detection and classification are crucial for pathological analysis, directly affecting diagnostic accuracy and treatment planning. To capture complex cellular interactions beyond visual appearance within the tumor microenvironment, several approaches have employed graph neural networks to model spatial and relational patterns among cell nuclei, yielding promising results. However, these methods typically adopt a two-stage paradigm of visual extraction followed by relational modeling, which necessitates separate tuning for each stage, thereby increasing pipeline complexity and hindering end-to-end joint optimization. In this paper, we propose an end-to-end framework for cell detection and classification that jointly models patch-level visual representations and instance-level interactions, which incorporates a dynamic graph construction module and an instance-aware graph network. Specifically, the graph construction module dynamically builds the graph structure using learnable queries derived from patch-level features as cell instance representations, with adjacency defined by integrating feature similarity and spatial distances. The instance-aware graph network performs adaptive instance filtering and feature reorganization, aggregating them over the cell graph into a topological latent state for a selective state-space transition driven by visual cues, fusing appearance and relational evidence. When evaluated on multiple datasets with different staining protocols for cell and nucleus detection, our method significantly outperforms existing approaches in both detection and classification performance. The code will be released at https://github.com/RuochenLiu23/IGM.

</details>


### [165] [Diffusion Trajectory Modeling for Semantic Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15357)
*Yusung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将扩散模型中每个空间patch随时间演化的中间表示视为轨迹进行建模，用于语义对应，优于只看单步或简单聚合的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多仅使用某一时刻的单个特征图或跨时刻的静态聚合，忽视了扩散过程中特征随时间的连续演化；作者观察到各空间patch在时间轴上形成具有语义意义的轨迹，包含静态快照难以捕获的语义。

Method: 提出Diffusion Trajectory Modeling (DTM)：把扩散过程中的中间特征视为时间序列，对每个空间patch构建跨多个timestep的轨迹，进行轨迹建模与匹配，从轨迹相似性中提取语义对应线索。

Result: 在SPair-71k、SPair-U与AP-10K数据集上取得强性能，实证显示空间上对应的patch在扩散时间轴上呈现相似轨迹模式，说明时间维度承载语义信息。

Conclusion: 将扩散表示从“静态快照”转为“轨迹中心”的建模视角，可更好地利用扩散中的时序语义以提升语义对应等下游任务表现。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate images through an iterative diffusion process, and recent studies have demonstrated that the intermediate feature maps produced during this process contain rich visual representations, leading to their adoption across a variety of downstream tasks. However, most existing approaches are limited to either using a single feature map at a specific timestep or aggregating feature maps across multiple timesteps. We observe that intermediate representations in the diffusion process form meaningful trajectories along the time axis.
